# ./commerce/server API declaration snapshot

Maturity: **experimental**.

Development-preview API; no stable compatibility claim.

Generated by `node --experimental-strip-types ./scripts/api-contract.ts --update`. Every change requires explicit review.

## dist/bff/dispatch.d.ts

```ts
import type { HttpRequest, HttpResponse } from "../http/index.js";
export type RouteEntry = {
    route: string;
    pattern: RegExp;
    params: string[];
    handler: (req: HttpRequest, res: HttpResponse) => unknown;
    /** Fail unsupported hosted bundles closed before auth without weakening direct-route auth. */
    availability?: "direct-postgres";
};
/** Match `routes` against one request and run the handler, or answer not found. */
export type BffDispatch = (routes: readonly RouteEntry[], req: HttpRequest, res: HttpResponse) => Promise<unknown>;
export interface BffDispatcherOptions {
    /** Environment read on each request to decide a route's bundle availability. */
    env: () => Record<string, string | undefined>;
    /** Writes the deployment's not-found response for an unmatched or unavailable path. */
    notFound: (res: HttpResponse, pathname: string) => void;
    /** Runs once per request before matching (for example, ambient request configuration). */
    beforeDispatch?: () => void;
    /**
     * Runs after matching and before the handler, for example route authorization; resolving false
     * means it already answered. Required, so no composition skips it by omission; a deployment
     * without guarded routes passes `() => true`.
     */
    preflight: (req: HttpRequest, res: HttpResponse, route: string) => Promise<boolean> | boolean;
}
/** Build the dispatcher a deployment mounts on its hosted and Node entrypoints alike. */
export declare function createBffDispatcher(options: BffDispatcherOptions): BffDispatch;
```
## dist/bff/envelope.d.ts

```ts
import type { HttpResponse } from "../http/index.js";
export declare const BFF_REQUEST_REFERENCE_HEADER = "x-request-id";
export declare const BFF_ERROR_CODES: readonly ["BAD_REQUEST", "UNAUTHORIZED", "FORBIDDEN", "NOT_FOUND", "METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED", "CONFLICT", "RATE_LIMITED", "UPSTREAM_UNAVAILABLE", "INTERNAL", "INVALID_RESPONSE"];
export type BffErrorCode = (typeof BFF_ERROR_CODES)[number];
export type BffMeta = {
    requestId?: string;
    contractVersion?: string;
    [key: string]: unknown;
};
export type BffError = {
    code: BffErrorCode;
    message: string;
    details?: unknown;
};
export type BffSuccessResponse<T> = {
    ok: true;
    data: T;
    meta?: BffMeta;
};
export type BffErrorResponse = {
    ok: false;
    error: BffError;
    meta?: BffMeta;
};
export type BffResponse<T> = BffSuccessResponse<T> | BffErrorResponse;
/** The HTTP status each error code answers with unless the caller names another. */
export declare const BFF_ERROR_STATUS: Readonly<Record<BffErrorCode, number>>;
export interface BffErrorOptions {
    status?: number;
    details?: unknown;
    meta?: BffMeta;
}
export interface BffSuccessOptions {
    cacheControl?: string;
}
export interface BffResponderOptions {
    /** The request reference observed for this response, if the deployment records one. */
    responseRequestId?: (res: HttpResponse) => string | undefined;
}
export interface BffResponder {
    sendBffSuccess<T>(res: HttpResponse, data: T, meta?: BffMeta, options?: BffSuccessOptions): void;
    sendBffError(res: HttpResponse, code: BffErrorCode, message: string, options?: BffErrorOptions): void;
    sendMethodNotAllowed(res: HttpResponse, allowedMethods: readonly string[]): void;
}
/** Build the envelope writers a deployment's handlers share. */
export declare function createBffResponder(options?: BffResponderOptions): BffResponder;
```

```ts
    contractVersion: string;
}> {
    responder: BffResponder;

    /** Builds this request's health payload: service identity, release provenance and the rest. */
    read: () => unknown;
}
export declare function createHealthHandler<T extends {
    contractVersion: string;
}>(options: HealthHandlerOptions<T>): (req: HttpRequest, res: HttpResponse) => void;
```

```ts
import type { HttpRequest, HttpResponse } from "../http/index.js";
export interface ObservedRequestContext {
    readonly requestId: string;
}
export interface RequestContextOptions {
    /** Chooses this request's reference. Called once per request. */
    readRequestId: (req: HttpRequest) => string;
}
export interface RequestContextStore {
    /** Create (or reuse) the request's context and attach it to the response. */
    install(req: HttpRequest, res: HttpResponse): ObservedRequestContext;
    /** The request's context, created on first use. */
    readOrCreate(req: HttpRequest): ObservedRequestContext;
    /** The context attached to a response by `install`, if any. */
    readResponse(res: HttpResponse): ObservedRequestContext | undefined;
}
export declare function createRequestContextStore(options: RequestContextOptions): RequestContextStore;
```

## dist/commerce/adapters/managed/commerceOmsPort.d.ts

```ts
import type { OmsActionAvailabilityFlags } from "../../model/omsReadModelHelpers.js";
import { type CommerceOmsHoldPort, type CommerceOmsReadPort } from "../../oms/omsPorts.js";
import type { OmsManagedReadProjections } from "./oms/readProjections.js";
import type { CommerceOmsRpcClient, CommerceOmsStore, RpcError } from "./oms/types.js";
export type { CommerceOmsRpcClient } from "./oms/types.js";
export declare function createManagedCommerceOmsPort(client: CommerceOmsRpcClient, options?: {
    actionFlags?: OmsActionAvailabilityFlags;
    queries?: Pick<CommerceOmsStore, "query">;
    projections?: OmsManagedReadProjections;
}): CommerceOmsReadPort & CommerceOmsHoldPort;
/**
 * Two outcomes, and the split is the honest one. An error naming a refusal this command can
 * reach - its own `commerce_oms_replacement_*` names, or the reservation vocabulary raised
 * through `inventory_reserve_order` while it mints the replacement's stock generation - is
 * something the operator caused and can answer: it becomes a conflict carrying only the
 * stable reason, not the SQLSTATE and not the message, because that reason is the single
 * field the operator surface renders from. Anything else never reached a refusal at all
 * (transport, permissions, a dead connection) and stays an upstream failure keyed by
 * SQLSTATE, which is diagnostic and never shown as a refusal.
 *
 * The reservation half is the one this wave added, and it is the most likely legitimate
 * refusal in production: the command reserves through an external stock authority, so a
 * stockout arrives as `inventory_external_provider_insufficient_available_stock`. It carries
 * no `commerce_oms_replacement_` prefix, matched nothing, and the operator was told to check
 * holds that were not there. The vocabulary itself lives in
 * `src/domains/commerce/omsPorts.ts` so the surface renders from the same list.
 */
export declare function mapReplacementRefusal(error: RpcError): Error;
```

```ts
/** Returns request/approve/reject only. Restock and refund fields record intent; no receive, restock or refund execution. */

    restockDisposition: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodEnum<{
        restock: "restock";
        quarantine: "quarantine";
        scrap: "scrap";
    }>>;
}, z.core.$strip>;
/** Bind host reason codes for request/approve/reject only; restock/refund fields record intent, without execution. */
export declare function createCommerceReturnsSchemas<const Reasons extends readonly [string, ...string[]]>(reasonCodes: Reasons): {
    returnRequestCreateSchema: z.ZodObject<{
        idempotencyKey: z.ZodString;
        orderId: z.ZodGUID;
        reasonCode: z.ZodEnum<{ [k_1 in Reasons[number]]: k_1; } extends infer T ? { [k in keyof T]: T[k]; } : never>;
        lines: z.ZodArray<z.ZodObject<{
            orderItemId: z.ZodGUID;
            sku: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodString>;
            quantity: z.ZodNumber;
            restockDisposition: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodEnum<{
                restock: "restock";
                quarantine: "quarantine";
                scrap: "scrap";
            }>>;
        }, z.core.$strip>>;
        customerNote: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodString>;
    }, z.core.$strip>;
};
/** Neutral default reasons for request/approve/reject only; restock/refund fields record intent, without execution. */
export declare const returnRequestCreateSchema: z.ZodObject<{
    idempotencyKey: z.ZodString;
    orderId: z.ZodGUID;
    reasonCode: z.ZodEnum<{
        other: "other";
        damaged: "damaged";
        wrong_item: "wrong_item";
        not_as_described: "not_as_described";
        changed_mind: "changed_mind";
    }>;
    lines: z.ZodArray<z.ZodObject<{
        orderItemId: z.ZodGUID;
        sku: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodString>;
        quantity: z.ZodNumber;
        restockDisposition: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodEnum<{
            restock: "restock";
            quarantine: "quarantine";
            scrap: "scrap";
        }>>;
    }, z.core.$strip>>;
    customerNote: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodString>;
}, z.core.$strip>;
/** Returns request/approve/reject only. Restock and refund fields record intent; no receive, restock or refund execution. */

/** Returns request/approve/reject only. Restock and refund fields record intent; no receive, restock or refund execution. */

    idempotencyKey: z.ZodString;
    returnRequestId: z.ZodGUID;
    adminNote: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodString>;
}, z.core.$strip>;
/** Returns request/approve/reject only. Restock and refund fields record intent; no receive, restock or refund execution. */
export type ReturnRequestCreateInput<Reason extends string = z.infer<typeof returnRequestCreateSchema>["reasonCode"]> = Omit<z.infer<typeof returnRequestCreateSchema>, "reasonCode"> & {
    reasonCode: Reason;
    requestedBy?: string | null;
};
/** Returns request/approve/reject only. Restock and refund fields record intent; no receive, restock or refund execution. */

/** Returns request/approve/reject only. Restock and refund fields record intent; no receive, restock or refund execution. */
export interface ReturnLifecycleResult {
    returnRequestId: string;
    status: string;
    replayed: boolean;
}
/** Returns request/approve/reject only. Restock and refund fields record intent; no receive, restock or refund execution. */
```

```ts
export type OrderMoneyIssueCode = "allocated_discount_mismatch" | "catalog_subtotal_mismatch" | "effective_subtotal_mismatch" | "header_equation_mismatch" | "item_catalog_equation_mismatch" | "item_catalog_money_out_of_range" | "item_discount_out_of_range" | "item_effective_money_out_of_range" | "item_gross_equation_mismatch" | "item_net_vat_mismatch" | "item_quantity_out_of_range" | "item_vat_rate_out_of_range" | "missing_item_canonical_money" | "mixed_vat_rates" | "product_discount_out_of_range" | "shipping_discount_out_of_range" | "tax_total_mismatch" | "total_positions_mismatch";
export interface OrderMoneyHeaderRow {
    id?: string;
    currency: string;
    subtotal_cents: number;
    discount_cents: number;
    shipping_cents: number;
    shipping_discount_cents: number;
    tax_cents: number;
    total_cents: number;
}

    quantity: number;
    unit_price_cents: number;
    total_cents: number;

    effective_total_cents: number | null | undefined;
    effective_net_cents: number | null | undefined;
    vat_rate_bps: number;
}

    id: string | null;
    quantity: number;
    catalogUnit: number;
    catalogTotal: number;
    discountAllocated: number;
    effectiveGross: number;
    effectiveNet: number;
    vatRateBps: number;
}

    currency: string;
    subtotal: number;
    productDiscount: number;
    shippingGross: number;
    shippingDiscount: number;
    shippingEffective: number;
    tax: number;
    total: number;
    lines: CanonicalOrderMoneyLine[];
    validationScope: "full" | "header";
    reconciled: boolean;
    issueCodes: OrderMoneyIssueCode[];
}
export interface OrderMoneyDiagnostic {
    event: "commerce_order_money_unreconciled";
    orderId: string | null;
    validationScope: CanonicalOrderMoney["validationScope"];
    issueCodes: OrderMoneyIssueCode[];
}
```

## dist/commerce/contracts/state.d.ts

```ts
export declare const CART_STATUSES: readonly ["draft"];
export declare const CHECKOUT_STATUSES: readonly ["not_configured", "created", "expired"];
export declare const ORDER_DRAFT_STATUSES: readonly ["draft"];
export declare const ORDER_STATUSES: readonly ["draft", "pending_payment", "paid", "failed", "expired", "fulfillment_pending", "cancelled", "fulfilled", "refunded"];
export declare const PAYMENT_STATUSES: readonly ["not_started", "requires_action", "processing", "paid", "failed", "expired", "refunded", "partially_refunded", "disputed"];
export declare const OMS_FULFILLMENT_BLOCK_REASONS: readonly ["order_not_paid", "payment_not_succeeded", "subscription_cycle_not_paid", "active_hold", "missing_shipping_address", "inventory_review", "risk_review"];
export declare const OMS_INVENTORY_STATUSES: readonly ["not_checked", "reserved", "missing", "released", "expired", "consumed", "review_required"];
export type CartStatus = (typeof CART_STATUSES)[number];
export type CheckoutStatus = (typeof CHECKOUT_STATUSES)[number];
export type OrderDraftStatus = (typeof ORDER_DRAFT_STATUSES)[number];
export type OrderStatus = (typeof ORDER_STATUSES)[number];
export type PaymentStatus = (typeof PAYMENT_STATUSES)[number];
export type OmsFulfillmentBlockReason = (typeof OMS_FULFILLMENT_BLOCK_REASONS)[number];
export type OmsInventoryStatus = (typeof OMS_INVENTORY_STATUSES)[number];
export interface CommerceFulfillmentEligibilityInput<PaymentState extends string = string, SubscriptionState extends string = string> {
    orderMode: "one_time" | "subscription_cycle";
    orderStatus: OrderStatus;
    paymentStatus: PaymentState;
    subscriptionCycleStatus?: SubscriptionState | null;
```

## dist/commerce/model/customerFulfillmentRecovery.d.ts

```ts
import type { CustomerFulfillmentStep } from "@openlup/core/fulfillment";
import type { ProviderStatusEvidence, ReleasedProviderExceptionHold, ResolvedProviderExceptionRecovery, TimedFulfillmentSignal } from "../contracts/fulfillmentRecovery.js";
export { stepForToken } from "@openlup/core/fulfillment";
export type { CustomerFulfillmentStep } from "@openlup/core/fulfillment";
export type { ProviderStatusEvidence, ReleasedProviderExceptionHold, ResolvedProviderExceptionRecovery, TimedFulfillmentSignal } from "../contracts/fulfillmentRecovery.js";
/** Server-only shape read from a released automatic provider-exception hold. */
/**
 * Finds only database-authored, trigger-created recovery links. The returned IDs
 * stay server-side: the browser receives the resulting Layer C step, never hold
 * metadata or provider evidence identifiers.
 */
export declare function resolveProviderExceptionRecovery(input: {
    automaticHoldSource?: string;
    automaticReleaseSource?: string;
    fulfillmentOrderId: string | null | undefined;
    releasedHolds: ReleasedProviderExceptionHold[];
    statusEvidence: ProviderStatusEvidence[];
}): ResolvedProviderExceptionRecovery;
/**
 * The customer step for an order that is terminal BY OUR OWN DECISION
 * (`cancelled` / `refunded`), or `null` when normal rank resolution applies.
 *
 * Rank alone is not enough here: `delivered` (7) outranks `cancelled` (6), so a
 * single provider `DELIVERED` evidence row on an order we cancelled would tell
 * the customer "delivered". Carrier evidence is a fact and stays recorded, but
 * it cannot un-cancel our own order, so the terminal order status wins.
 *
 * This is a ceiling, not a new opinion: `delivered` is the ONLY step ranking
 * above `cancelled`, and the order's own status already contributes `cancelled`
 * to the ranked signals, so every non-delivered outcome is unchanged. The canon's
 * ranks stay untouched — they are shared with OMS health, observability and the
 * admin timeline, where provider-versus-local divergence must remain visible.
 * Single owner of this rule: the customer read model and the browser fallback
 * resolver both call it instead of each keeping a copy.
 */
export declare function customerStepForTerminalOrder(orderStatus: string | null | undefined): CustomerFulfillmentStep | null;
/** Resolve rank normally; only a uniquely newer canonical timed exception may cover delivered. */
export declare function customerStepFromTimedSignals(baseTokens: Array<string | null | undefined>, evidence: TimedFulfillmentSignal[], recovery?: ResolvedProviderExceptionRecovery): CustomerFulfillmentStep;
/** Keep legacy/provider-only Provider CANCELLED evidence customer-safe. */
export declare function customerSafeEvidenceToken(localStatus: string | null | undefined, providerStatus: string | null | undefined): string | null;
```

```ts
/**
 * The order's replacement chain, detail-only.
 *
 * ⛔ This is NOT a second fulfilment summary. `fulfillment` stays exactly what W3 collapsed it
 * to - one row, the parcel currently representing the order, with every companion narrowed to
 * that parcel's id. Widening those companions was measured and rejected: the dispatch refs,
 * provider attempts and status evidence of a superseded parcel would then be read by the
 * fulfilment health classifier, the fulfilment debug panel and the customer-step derivation,
 * each of which would answer for the wrong parcel - a dispatch status of `created` for a
 * replacement that has never been dispatched, or a customer step of `delivered` for a parcel
 * that is still `created`.
 *
 * So the superseded parcels carry their own, deliberately small payload: what a support agent
 * needs to answer "where did the first one go?" and to file a carrier claim. Tracking number,
 * carrier, service, the two handover instants, and the parcel's own status.
 */

        handed_over: "handed_over";

    handedOverAt: z.ZodNullable<z.ZodString>;
```

## dist/commerce/oms/omsPorts.d.ts

```ts
import type { OmsReplacementRefusal } from "../contracts/omsTypes.js";
import { type OmsCurrencyPolicy } from "../model/omsCurrency.js";
export type { CommerceOmsHoldPort, CommerceOmsReadPort, OmsReplacementRefusal } from "../contracts/omsTypes.js";
/**
 * Every refusal `commerce_oms_request_replacement_shipment` can reach an operator with,
 * as one closed vocabulary shared by the adapter that classifies it and the admin surface
 * that renders a sentence for it. Two lists would drift, and the failure mode of drift is
 * the operator reading advice about a hold when the warehouse is simply out of stock.
 *
 * The first group is the command's own `commerce_oms_replacement_*` names. The second is
 * the reservation vocabulary raised THROUGH it: the command mints a fresh generation via
 * `inventory_reserve_order`, and on a stock authority outside this system that call is the
 * most likely legitimate refusal there is. Those errors never carried the command's prefix,
 * so before this list they matched nothing, degraded to an upstream failure, and the
 * operator was told to check holds that were not there.
 *
 * It stays an allowlist, not a translation table: a reason absent from it degrades to
 * `unspecified_refusal`, so no database text can travel to a response, a log or a screen.
 *
 * ⛔ The exported names deliberately omit the `CommerceOms` prefix every other symbol in
 * this file carries. `src/lib/commerceOmsBoundary.test.ts` forbids that token in production
 * UI files outside a small allowlist, and the admin surface imports these three symbols.
 * Renaming them is the honest way to keep that boundary guard intact; widening its allowlist
 * to admit one more file would weaken the control instead of respecting it.
 */
export declare const OMS_REPLACEMENT_COMMAND_REFUSALS: readonly ["invalid_input", "idempotency_conflict", "order_not_found", "order_not_replaceable", "missing_client", "missing_shipping_address", "shipping_address_not_found", "payment_not_succeeded", "subscription_cycle_not_paid", "no_parcel_to_replace", "undispatched_replacement_exists", "missing_order_items", "missing_catalog_sku", "missing_inventory_reservation", "blocked_by_payment_not_succeeded_hold", "blocked_by_inventory_review_hold", "blocked_by_risk_review_hold", "blocked_by_address_review_hold", "blocked_by_manual_support_hold"];
export declare const OMS_REPLACEMENT_RESERVATION_REFUSALS: readonly ["inventory_external_provider_insufficient_available_stock", "inventory_external_provider_stock_stale", "inventory_external_provider_stock_missing", "inventory_external_provider_sku_not_found", "inventory_external_provider_location_missing", "inventory_reservation_insufficient_available_stock", "inventory_reservation_allocation_incomplete", "inventory_reservation_payment_status_not_reservable", "inventory_reservation_invalid_quantity"];
export declare const UNSPECIFIED_REPLACEMENT_REFUSAL = "unspecified_refusal";
export declare const OMS_REPLACEMENT_REFUSALS: readonly ["invalid_input", "idempotency_conflict", "order_not_found", "order_not_replaceable", "missing_client", "missing_shipping_address", "shipping_address_not_found", "payment_not_succeeded", "subscription_cycle_not_paid", "no_parcel_to_replace", "undispatched_replacement_exists", "missing_order_items", "missing_catalog_sku", "missing_inventory_reservation", "blocked_by_payment_not_succeeded_hold", "blocked_by_inventory_review_hold", "blocked_by_risk_review_hold", "blocked_by_address_review_hold", "blocked_by_manual_support_hold", "inventory_external_provider_insufficient_available_stock", "inventory_external_provider_stock_stale", "inventory_external_provider_stock_missing", "inventory_external_provider_sku_not_found", "inventory_external_provider_location_missing", "inventory_reservation_insufficient_available_stock", "inventory_reservation_allocation_incomplete", "inventory_reservation_payment_status_not_reservable", "inventory_reservation_invalid_quantity", "unspecified_refusal"];
/**
 * Reads the refusal a replacement error carries, from any of the shapes a BFF error can
 * legitimately arrive in. Returns null when the text names no refusal this vocabulary
 * knows, which is the honest answer: the operator gets the generic sentence plus the raw
 * detail to hand to the team, never a guess dressed as advice.
 */
export declare function readOmsReplacementRefusal(text: string): OmsReplacementRefusal | null;
export declare class CommerceOmsInvalidResponseError extends Error {
    constructor();
}
export declare class CommerceOmsPersistenceError extends Error {
    readonly details: Record<string, unknown>;
    constructor(message?: string, details?: Record<string, unknown>);
}
/**
 * An order row came back from the database in a currency this deployment does
 * not accept. The channel ingest rail is deliberately multi-currency, so such a
 * row can genuinely exist; what it cannot do is reach the read model, whose
 * money nodes are pinned to the platform's accepted set.
 *
 * A subclass rather than a new error family, so it inherits the persistence
 * error's HTTP mapping untouched: this is still an upstream read that could not
 * be served, and the operator-facing status must not move. The gain is entirely
 * diagnostic - the failure now names the orders and the codes instead of
 * surfacing as an anonymous validation error fifteen frames later.
 */
export declare class CommerceOmsUnsupportedCurrencyError extends CommerceOmsPersistenceError {
    readonly orderIds: readonly string[];
    readonly currencies: readonly string[];
    constructor(offenders: ReadonlyArray<{
        orderId: string;
        currency: string;
    }>);
}
/**
 * Guards the one field of an order row that can break the OMS read.
 *
 * This is deliberately not a schema for the whole row. Of that row's ~22
 * columns exactly one - `currency` - is both able to fail the read model and
 * something the platform holds an opinion about; the rest would cost a new
 * contract surface parsed once per row per page, and would only be coherent if
 * the other cast row kinds on this path were validated too.
 *
 * It also deliberately refuses rather than filtering. Dropping the offending
 * row would keep the page rendering while hiding a real customer order from the
 * operator handling it - a worse failure than a named refusal.
 */
export declare function assertOmsOrderRowCurrency(rows: ReadonlyArray<{
    id: string;
    currency: string;
}>, currency?: OmsCurrencyPolicy): void;
export declare class CommerceOmsConflictError extends Error {
    readonly details: Record<string, unknown>;
    constructor(message?: string, details?: Record<string, unknown>);
}
```

## dist/commerce/orderReviewPorts.d.ts

```ts
import type { OrderReview, OrderReviewAdminDetail, OrderReviewMedia, OrderReviewMediaConfirm, OrderReviewMediaIntent, OrderReviewMediaIntentResult, OrderReviewModeration, OrderReviewReadResult, OrderReviewSubmit } from "./orderReviewContracts.js";
export type Replay<T> = {
    value: T;
    replayed: boolean;
};
/** Server-only storage locator. It is not a public schema or BFF DTO. */
export type OrderReviewMediaObject = {
    media: OrderReviewMedia;
    objectKey: string;
};
/** A live raw-upload lease; the clear capability itself is deliberately absent. */
export type OrderReviewUploadReservation = OrderReviewMediaObject & {
    leaseVersion: number;
    leaseExpiresAt: string;
    declaredContentType: "image/jpeg" | "image/png" | "image/webp";
    declaredByteLength: number;
};
/** Physical deletion is fenced by a durable, non-public cleanup receipt. */
export type OrderReviewMediaCleanup = OrderReviewMediaObject & {
    tempKey?: string;
    cleanupReceipt: string;
    replayed: boolean;
};
export type OrderReviewUploadAdmission = {
    kind: "unavailable";
} | {
    kind: "conflict";
} | {
    kind: "lease";
    reservation: OrderReviewUploadReservation;
} | {
    kind: "complete";
    media: OrderReviewMedia;
};
/** Claimed by one worker; no worker may scan a volume or infer an object key. */
export type OrderReviewMediaReconcileTask = {
    receipt: string;
    kind: "finalize" | "delete";
    objectKey: string;
    tempKey?: string;
    expectedDeclaredContentType: "image/jpeg" | "image/png" | "image/webp";
    expectedDeclaredByteLength: number;
};
export type OrderReviewMediaReconcileOutcome = {
    kind: "stored";
    observedContentType: "image/jpeg" | "image/png" | "image/webp";
    observedByteLength: number;
    observedDigest: string;
} | {
    kind: "deleted";
} | {
    kind: "retry";
    reason: string;
};
export interface OrderReviewLifecyclePort {
    /** null means an invalid/revoked grant; { review: null } is an eligible first submission. */
    read(grant: string): Promise<OrderReviewReadResult | null>;
    submit(input: OrderReviewSubmit): Promise<Replay<OrderReview>>;
    createMediaIntent(input: OrderReviewMediaIntent): Promise<OrderReviewMediaIntentResult>;
    confirmMedia(input: OrderReviewMediaConfirm): Promise<Replay<OrderReviewMedia>>;
    moderate(input: OrderReviewModeration & {
        actorRef: string;
    }): Promise<Replay<OrderReview>>;
    revoke(input: {
        reviewRef: string;
        actorRef: string;
        idempotencyKey: string;
    }): Promise<{
        replayed: boolean;
    }>;
}
/** Metadata-only seam. Lane B owns bytes and can never resolve a grant directly. */
export interface OrderReviewMediaPort {
    /** The route may expose only .media; .objectKey is for the object-store adapter. */
    readAuthorized(grant: string, mediaRef: string): Promise<OrderReviewMediaObject | null>;
    /**
     * Raw ingress supplies only SHA-256(clear capability), never grant/order/media
     * identifiers. The implementation must lock and re-check grant -> review ->
     * media while admitting this live lease.
     */
    admitUpload(input: {
        capabilityDigest: string;
        declaredContentType: "image/jpeg" | "image/png" | "image/webp";
        declaredByteLength: number;
        writerRef: string;
    }): Promise<OrderReviewUploadAdmission>;
    /** The transaction remains open across promote; a stale grant/lease cannot rename. */
    finalizeUpload(input: {
        capabilityDigest: string;
        writerRef: string;
        leaseVersion: number;
        digest: string;
        byteLength: number;
        promote: () => Promise<void>;
    }): Promise<OrderReviewMedia | null>;
    abortUpload(input: {
        capabilityDigest: string;
        writerRef: string;
        leaseVersion: number;
        reason: string;
    }): Promise<void>;
    readAdmin(input: {
        actorRef: string;
        mediaRef: string;
    }): Promise<OrderReviewMediaObject | null>;
    /** Marks cleanup_pending and returns the exact object key plus durable receipt. */
    prepareAdminDelete(input: {
        actorRef: string;
        mediaRef: string;
        idempotencyKey: string;
    }): Promise<OrderReviewMediaCleanup | null>;
    /** May mark deleted only after object storage confirms physical removal. */
    completeAdminDelete(input: {
        actorRef: string;
        cleanupReceipt: string;
    }): Promise<{
        replayed: boolean;
    }>;
    /** Claims bounded exact keys under SKIP LOCKED; B owns physical inspection/deletion. */
    claimReconcile(limit: number): Promise<OrderReviewMediaReconcileTask[]>;
    /** Fenced completion cannot overwrite a later lease or a different object outcome. */
    completeReconcile(input: {
        receipt: string;
        outcome: OrderReviewMediaReconcileOutcome;
    }): Promise<void>;
}
```

```ts
import type { OmsHandlerDeps, OmsHandlerRequest } from "./handlerSupport.js";
/**
 * The `?view=control_plane_v1` read handlers. Unlike the V0 handlers in
 * `commerceOmsHandlers.ts`, these deliberately take NO `governance` dependency and
 * do not run `applyOmsAgentCustomerReadGate`: `commerce.oms_control_plane.v1`
 * carries no customer identity, so the gate would audit a `customer_read` of zero
 * customers and mask a field the strict contract forbids — and the portable
 * `node-postgres` branch of this same contract has neither an actor kind nor an
 * audit client to run it with. The exemption holds only while the projection stays
 * customer-free, a precondition pinned in `omsControlPlaneReadGovernance.test.ts`.
 */
import { type CommerceOmsControlPlanePort } from "../oms/omsControlPlane.js";
type Deps<Response> = OmsHandlerDeps<Response> & {
    port: CommerceOmsControlPlanePort;
};
export declare function createCommerceOmsControlPlaneListHandler<Response>({ authorizeAdmin, port, respond }: Deps<Response>): (req: OmsHandlerRequest, res: Response) => Promise<void>;
export declare function createCommerceOmsControlPlaneDetailHandler<Response>({ authorizeAdmin, port, respond }: Deps<Response>): (req: OmsHandlerRequest, res: Response) => Promise<void>;
export {};
```

```ts
import type { ZodType } from "zod";
import type { OutboxEventRow, OutboxHandler, OutboxHandlerOutcome } from "../outboxDispatchContracts.js";
import type { OrderEmailTotals, OrderPaidRawData, TransactionalEmailSendOutcome } from "../outboxOrderDraftEmailPorts.js";
import type { OrderEmailLinePresentation } from "../orderEmailLines.js";
export interface TransactionalEmailHandlerSpec<Payload, Recipient> {
    eventType: string;
    timeoutMs: number;
    templateSlug: string;
    schema: ZodType<Payload>;
    resolveKey(payload: Payload): string;
    recipientPort: {
        resolve(key: string, signal: AbortSignal): Promise<Recipient | null>;
    };
    findExistingSend(templateSlug: string, outboxEventId: string): Promise<boolean>;
    send(ctx: {
        row: OutboxEventRow;
        payload: Payload;
        recipient: Recipient;
        signal: AbortSignal;
    }): Promise<TransactionalEmailSendOutcome>;
}
/** Host bindings to the communications owner and presentation/provider policy. */
export interface CommerceEmailRuntime<Locale extends string = string> extends OrderEmailLinePresentation<Locale> {
    parseOutboxPayload<Payload>(schema: ZodType<Payload>, payload: unknown): {
        ok: true;
        data: Payload;
    } | {
        ok: false;
        outcome: OutboxHandlerOutcome;
    };
    mapSendOutcome(outcome: TransactionalEmailSendOutcome): OutboxHandlerOutcome;
    createTransactionalEmailHandler<Payload, Recipient>(spec: TransactionalEmailHandlerSpec<Payload, Recipient>): OutboxHandler;
    resolveLocale(country: string | null | undefined): Locale;
    /** null preserves ordinary totals and item prices; a presentation suppresses item prices. */
    presentPaidOrder(data: OrderPaidRawData): OrderEmailTotals | null;
    providerOwnsDeliveredNotification(providerKind: string): boolean;
    normalizeCarrierKind(input: {
        carrierKind: string | null;
        service: string | null;
    }): string | null;
    carrierTrackingUrl(carrierKind: string | null, trackingNumber: string): string | null;
    legacyTrackingUrl(ref: {
        providerKind: string | null;
        carrierKind: string | null;
        trackingNumber: string;
    }, hasExplicitReferences: boolean): string | null;
}
```

```ts
};

        }>;

}
```

```ts
import { type OrderReviewFailure, type OrderReviewReadResult } from "../orderReviewContracts.js";
import type { OrderReviewLifecyclePort } from "../orderReviewPorts.js";
type Result<T> = {
    ok: true;
    value: T;
    replayed?: boolean;
} | {
    ok: false;
    error: OrderReviewFailure;
};
/** HTTP-neutral customer orchestration; route composition owns bearer extraction. */
export declare function createOrderReviewHandlers(port: OrderReviewLifecyclePort): {
    read(grant: string): Promise<Result<OrderReviewReadResult>>;
    submit(input: unknown): Promise<{
        ok: false;
        error: OrderReviewFailure;
    } | {
        ok: true;
        value: {
            contractVersion: "commerce.order-review.v1";
            reviewRef: string;
            rating: number;
            comment: string | null;
            moderation: "pending" | "visible" | "hidden";
            createdAt: string;
        };
        replayed: boolean | undefined;
    }>;
    createMediaIntent(input: unknown): Promise<{
        ok: false;
        error: OrderReviewFailure;
    } | {
        ok: true;
        value: {
            media: {
                state: "pending";
                observedContentType: null;
                observedByteLength: null;
                observedDigest: null;
                mediaRef: string;
                declaredContentType: "image/png" | "image/jpeg" | "image/webp";
                declaredByteLength: number;
            } | {
                state: "stored" | "confirmed";
                observedContentType: "image/png" | "image/jpeg" | "image/webp";
                observedByteLength: number;
                observedDigest: string;
                mediaRef: string;
                declaredContentType: "image/png" | "image/jpeg" | "image/webp";
                declaredByteLength: number;
            } | {
                state: "cleanup_pending" | "deleted";
                observedContentType: "image/png" | "image/jpeg" | "image/webp" | null;
                observedByteLength: number | null;
                observedDigest: string | null;
                mediaRef: string;
                declaredContentType: "image/png" | "image/jpeg" | "image/webp";
                declaredByteLength: number;
            };
            uploadExpiresAt: string;
            replayed: boolean;
        };
        replayed: boolean | undefined;
    }>;
    confirmMedia(input: unknown): Promise<{
        ok: false;
        error: OrderReviewFailure;
    } | {
        ok: true;
        value: {
            state: "pending";
            observedContentType: null;
            observedByteLength: null;
            observedDigest: null;
            mediaRef: string;
            declaredContentType: "image/png" | "image/jpeg" | "image/webp";
            declaredByteLength: number;
        } | {
            state: "stored" | "confirmed";
            observedContentType: "image/png" | "image/jpeg" | "image/webp";
            observedByteLength: number;
            observedDigest: string;
            mediaRef: string;
            declaredContentType: "image/png" | "image/jpeg" | "image/webp";
            declaredByteLength: number;
        } | {
            state: "cleanup_pending" | "deleted";
            observedContentType: "image/png" | "image/jpeg" | "image/webp" | null;
            observedByteLength: number | null;
            observedDigest: string | null;
            mediaRef: string;
            declaredContentType: "image/png" | "image/jpeg" | "image/webp";
            declaredByteLength: number;
        };
        replayed: boolean | undefined;
    }>;
};
/** Shared command scaffold: parse result, one port call, the value mapper, failure mapping. */
export declare function runOrderReviewCommand<D, R extends {
    replayed?: boolean;
}, V>(parsed: {
    success: true;
    data: D;
} | {
    success: false;
}, call: (data: D) => Promise<R>, map: (result: R) => V): Promise<{
    ok: false;
    error: OrderReviewFailure;
} | {
    ok: true;
    value: V;
    replayed: boolean | undefined;
}>;
export {};
```

## dist/commerce/server/outboxDispatchRegistry.d.ts

```ts
import type { CommerceEmailRuntime } from "./emailRuntime.js";
import type { OutboxHandlerDescriptor } from "@openlup/core/outbox";
import type { OutboxHandler, OutboxHandlerRegistry } from "../outboxDispatchContracts.js";
import type { OrderPaymentLifecyclePort, OrderPaidLinesPort, OrderRecipientPort, TransactionalEmailPort } from "../outboxOrderDraftEmailPorts.js";
import { type OrderPaidParcelNoteReader } from "./outboxOrderPaidEmailHandler.js";
import { type DeliveredEmailProviderKindReader } from "./outboxShipmentDeliveredEmailHandler.js";
import type { OrderPaidFulfillmentPort } from "./outboxOrderPaidFulfillmentPorts.js";
import { type OrderPaidRiskAssessmentPort } from "./outboxOrderPaidFulfillmentHandler.js";
import { type OrderBuyerCommsPolicy } from "../salesChannelEnvelope.js";
/**
 * Reads the sales-source buyer-comms policy for one order (wave B6). `null` is
 * the answer for an order that names no channel — a storefront order, or one
 * that predates the source axis.
 *
 * The read is LIVE on every event, deliberately uncached: an operator who flips
 * `sales_channels.buyer_comms_owner` is answering a live complaint, and a cached
 * policy would keep sending for the rest of the process lifetime.
 */
export interface OrderBuyerCommsPolicyReader {
    readOrderBuyerCommsPolicy(orderUuid: string): Promise<OrderBuyerCommsPolicy | null>;
}
export type TransactionalEmailRegistryDeps<Locale extends string = string> = {
    runtime: CommerceEmailRuntime<Locale>;
    emailPort: TransactionalEmailPort<Locale>;
    recipientPort: OrderRecipientPort;
    lifecyclePort: OrderPaymentLifecyclePort;
    orderPaidLinesPort: OrderPaidLinesPort;
    orderPaidParcelNote?: OrderPaidParcelNoteReader<Locale>;
    deliveredProviderKindReader?: DeliveredEmailProviderKindReader;
    buyerCommsPolicyReader?: OrderBuyerCommsPolicyReader;
};
export declare function describeCoreOutboxHandlers(selection: {
    transactionalEmail: boolean;
    fulfillmentEnabled: boolean;
}): OutboxHandlerDescriptor[];
export declare function createOutboxDispatchRegistry<Locale extends string>(deps: {
    /** Bind the existing outbox rail's composition; commerce owns no second rail. */
    uniqueRegistry(handlers: readonly OutboxHandler[]): OutboxHandlerRegistry;
    transactionalEmail?: TransactionalEmailRegistryDeps<Locale>;
    fulfillmentPort?: OrderPaidFulfillmentPort;
    riskPort?: OrderPaidRiskAssessmentPort;
    riskEnabled?: boolean;
    fulfillmentEnabled: boolean;
    extraHandlers?: readonly OutboxHandler[];
    expectedDescriptors?: () => readonly OutboxHandlerDescriptor[];
}): OutboxHandlerRegistry;
```

## dist/fulfillment/contracts/alignment.d.ts

```ts
/**
 * Late delivery extends the next cycle, never stacks deliveries or moves it earlier.
 * A replacement may settle alignment as aligned only after reaching the customer.
 * Its superseded predecessor is no longer outstanding; an in-flight or undelivered
 * replacement still is. Never call subscription_delivery_alignment_confirm_replacement
 * for a replacement that did not arrive: it records "Parcel delivered" and shifts
 * the cycle later. That note-only operator attestation remains outside this port;
 * no automatic caller or new guard is introduced.
 */
export interface DeliveryAlignmentPort {
    recordArrival(input: {
        requestKey: string;
        parcelRef: string;
        deliveredAt: string;
        evidenceRef: `evidence:${string}`;
    }): Promise<{
        disposition: "aligned" | "outstanding" | "manual_review";
        nextCycleAt: string | null;
        replayed: boolean;
    }>;
    read(fulfillmentRef: string): Promise<{
        disposition: "aligned" | "outstanding" | "manual_review";
        nextCycleAt: string | null;
    }>;
}
```

```ts
import type { FulfillmentCapabilityDescriptor } from './capabilities.js';
import type { FulfillmentDispatchPort, FulfillmentDispatchRequest, FulfillmentRefusal, FulfillmentSelection } from './dispatch.js';
import type { FulfillmentNativeUnsupported } from './nativeUnsupported.js';
import type { DispatchRecord, FulfillmentStorePort } from './store.js';
/** In-memory ports and host closures only; never serialize options or recipient inputs. */
export interface FulfillmentServicesOptions {
    enabled?: boolean;
    selection: FulfillmentSelection | null;
    capability: FulfillmentCapabilityDescriptor | null;
    store: FulfillmentStorePort | null;
    provider: FulfillmentDispatchPort | null;
    readiness(): Promise<{
        ready: true;
    } | {
        ready: false;
        refusal: FulfillmentRefusal | FulfillmentNativeUnsupported;
    }>;
    listCandidates(limit: number): Promise<readonly FulfillmentDispatchRequest[]>;
    keys: {
        request(candidate: FulfillmentDispatchRequest): string;
        mappedAttempt(requestKey: string, mappedAttemptIndex: number): string;
        acceptance(fulfillmentRef: string): {
            providerAttemptKey: string;
            labelKey: string;
        };
    };
    config: {
        mode: DispatchRecord['mode'];
        batchLimit: number;
    };
    now?: () => Date;
}
/** Import and construction are inert. Every invocation checks activation before any worker. */
export interface FulfillmentServices {
    readiness(): ReturnType<FulfillmentServicesOptions['readiness']>;
    runDispatch(): Promise<FulfillmentNativeUnsupported | {
        kind: 'refused';
        refusal: FulfillmentRefusal;
    } | {
        ok: boolean;
        skipped: boolean;
        checked: number;
        updated: number;
        failures: number;
        mode: DispatchRecord['mode'];
        replayed: number;
        providerCalls: number;
        readBacks: number;
        reason: string | undefined;
    }>;
}
```

```ts
import type { FulfillmentRefusal } from "./dispatch.js";
export interface FulfillmentCapabilityDescriptor {
    providerRef: string;
    role: "warehouse" | "direct_carrier" | "manual" | "simulator";
    operations: Readonly<Record<"dispatch" | "readback" | "exactAbsence" | "verifyObservation" | "syncStock" | "syncProducts" | "labelCreate" | "labelRead" | "labelVoid" | "cancel" | "pickupPoint" | "courierPickup", {
        supported: true;
    } | {
        supported: false;
        reason: "not_exposed" | "host_only" | "not_implemented";
    }>>;
    authentication: "token" | "authenticated_timestamp" | "none";
    freshness: "authenticated_timestamp" | "not_available";
    defaultOff: true;
    selected: boolean;
    readiness: {
        ready: true;
    } | {
        ready: false;
        refusal: FulfillmentRefusal;
    };
    ownsDeliveredNotification: boolean;
    stockAuthority: "local_atp" | "external_stock_master_with_local_reservations";
    labelReadOwner: "host_storage" | "adapter" | "none";
    actionPolicy: {
        providerSpecificAdminActions: boolean;
        genericAdminActions: boolean;
        mutationSurface: "none" | "local_only" | "provider_adapter";
    };
}
```

## dist/fulfillment/contracts/conformance.d.ts

```ts
import type { FulfillmentCapabilityDescriptor } from "./capabilities.js";
import type { FulfillmentDispatchPort, FulfillmentDispatchRequest } from "./dispatch.js";
import type { FulfillmentLabelPort } from "./label.js";
import type { FulfillmentObservationPort, FulfillmentObservationCodecs } from "./observation.js";
import type { FulfillmentReadbackPort } from "./readback.js";
import type { FulfillmentStockSyncProvider, FulfillmentProductSyncProvider } from "./sync.js";
import type { FulfillmentStorePort } from "./store.js";
type Fixture<Method extends (...args: never[]) => unknown> = {
    input: Parameters<Method>;
    expected: Awaited<ReturnType<Method>>;
};
type SyncFixture<Port> = {
    [K in keyof Port]: Port[K] extends (...args: never[]) => unknown ? {
        operation: K;
        input: Parameters<Port[K]>;
        expected: Awaited<ReturnType<Port[K]>> | {
            kind: "refused";
            refusal: {
                reason: "unsupported";
                phase: "sync";
                retryable: false;
            };
        };
    } : never;
}[keyof Port];
/** Offline adapter-owned inputs and exact expected outputs; never real customer data. */
export interface FulfillmentAdapterConformanceSubject {
    approveTrackingUrl?(url: string): boolean;
    observationCodecs?: FulfillmentObservationCodecs;
    subjectId: string;
    adapter: {
        capabilities: FulfillmentCapabilityDescriptor;
        dispatch: FulfillmentDispatchPort;
        label?: FulfillmentLabelPort;
        observation?: FulfillmentObservationPort;
        readback?: FulfillmentReadbackPort;
        stockSync?: FulfillmentStockSyncProvider;
        productSync?: FulfillmentProductSyncProvider;
    };
    /** Count external submission I/O, including attempted calls. Preparation/refusals must not increase it. */
    readIoCount(): number;
    sensitiveValues: readonly string[];
    fixtures: {
        dispatch: readonly {
            input: FulfillmentDispatchRequest;
            expectedPrepare: Awaited<ReturnType<FulfillmentDispatchPort["prepare"]>>;
            expectedDispatch?: Awaited<ReturnType<FulfillmentDispatchPort["dispatch"]>>;
            /** Repeating this operation must not increase external submission I/O. */
            replay?: boolean;
        }[];
        label?: {
            create?: readonly Fixture<FulfillmentLabelPort["create"]>[];
            read?: readonly Fixture<FulfillmentLabelPort["read"]>[];
            void?: readonly Fixture<FulfillmentLabelPort["void"]>[];
        };
        observation?: readonly Fixture<FulfillmentObservationPort["verifyAndNormalize"]>[];
        readback?: readonly (Fixture<FulfillmentReadbackPort["read"]> & {
            localMissing?: boolean;
        })[];
        stockSync?: readonly SyncFixture<FulfillmentStockSyncProvider>[];
        productSync?: readonly SyncFixture<FulfillmentProductSyncProvider>[];
    };
    /** A fresh isolated host store per call; no database implementation is supplied by the runner. */
    store?: {
        create(): Promise<FulfillmentStorePort & {
            /** Offline fixture inspection, not a new production store operation. */
            readMappedAttempts(): Promise<readonly {
                attemptKey: string;
                fulfillmentRef: string;
                preparedEvidence: Parameters<FulfillmentStorePort["recordProviderAttempt"]>[2];
                mode: Parameters<FulfillmentStorePort["recordProviderAttempt"]>[3];
                status: "recorded";
            }[]>;
        }>;
        claim: Parameters<FulfillmentStorePort["claim"]>[0];
        remappedClaim: Parameters<FulfillmentStorePort["claim"]>[0];
        mappedAttemptKey: string;
        remappedAttemptKey: string;
        request: FulfillmentDispatchRequest;
        remappedRequest: FulfillmentDispatchRequest;
    };
}
/** Sanitized proof only: no fixture inputs, raw responses, transient URLs or credentials. */
export interface FulfillmentAdapterConformanceEvidence {
    subjects: readonly {
        subjectId: string;
        providerRef: string;
    }[];
    cases: readonly {
        subjectId: string;
        operation: string;
        passed: true;
    }[];
}
export {};
```

## dist/fulfillment/contracts/dispatch.d.ts

```ts
type DeliveryKind = "parcel-locker" | "courier";
type PickupPoint = {
    id: string;
    provider: string;
    name: string;
    address: {
        line1: string;
        postalCode: string;
        city: string;
        country: string;
    };
};
export interface FulfillmentSelection {
    providerRef: string | null;
    carrierRef: string | null;
    serviceRef: string | null;
    deliveryKind: DeliveryKind;
    pickupPoint: PickupPoint | null;
}
export interface FulfillmentRefusal {
    reason: "not_dispatchable" | "selection_required" | "provider_mismatch" | "carrier_service_required" | "disabled" | "not_ready" | "unsupported" | "withheld" | "claim_conflict" | "contact_stale" | "stock_contention" | "local_missing" | "io_failure" | "malformed" | "untrusted" | "unsupported_event" | "stale" | "missing_correlation";
    phase: "selection" | "prepare" | "claim" | "begin" | "dispatch" | "acknowledge" | "verify" | "normalize" | "apply" | "readback" | "sync";
    retryable: boolean;
}
/** Frozen domain input. Contact PII stays transient; only allowlisted mapped items enter evidence. Credentials stay in adapter closures. */
export interface FulfillmentDispatchRequest {
    requestKey: string;
    fulfillmentRef: string;
    orderRef: string;
    orderNumber: string | null;
    parcelRef: string | null;
    contactRevision: {
        schemaVersion: number;
        source: string;
        revision: number;
    };
    recipient: {
        name: string | null;
        email: string | null;
        phone: string | null;
        line1: string;
        line2: string | null;
        city: string;
        postalCode: string;
        country: string;
        deliveryInstructions: string | null;
        courierInstructions: string | null;
    };
    lines: readonly {
        skuRef: string;
        title: string | null;
        quantity: number;
        productRef: string | null;
        packRef: string | null;
        lotRef?: string | null;
        expirationDate?: string | null;
    }[];
    selection: FulfillmentSelection;
}
/** Allowlisted durable evidence; fingerprint commits to actual adapter command bytes. */
export interface DispatchEvidence {
    redactedFieldCount?: number;
    commandFingerprint: string;
    contactRevision: FulfillmentDispatchRequest["contactRevision"];
    providerRef: string;
    carrierRef: string | null;
    serviceRef: string | null;
    transcript?: {
        itemCount: number;
        items: readonly {
            skuRef: string;
            quantity: number;
            lotRef: string | null;
            expirationDate: string | null;
        }[];
        deliveryKind: DeliveryKind;
        pickupPointRef: string | null;
        /** Count of populated reference/date fields redacted by the durable projection. */
        redactedFieldCount?: number;
    };
    mapping?: {
        source: "dispatch_worker";
        mode: "shadow" | "stage" | "live";
        shadowMode: boolean;
    };
}
export interface FulfillmentOperationError {
    redactedFieldCount?: number;
    code: string;
    status: number | null;
    retryable: boolean;
    mayHaveSucceeded: boolean;
    /** Whether provider submission was attempted; absent means unknown, never proven false. */
    submissionAttempted?: boolean;
    /** Whether the HTTP response body was read, including empty or malformed JSON; absent means no body-read evidence. */
    responseBodyRead?: boolean;
    /** Machine cause only; never an arbitrary exception message. */
    cause?: string;
}
export type FulfillmentDispatchResult = ({
    error?: FulfillmentOperationError;
    redactedFieldCount?: number;
} & ({
    kind: "accepted";
    externalRef: string;
    evidence: DispatchEvidence;
    providerAttemptKey: string;
    labelKey: string;
    recovery?: {
        source: "persisted_acceptance";
        proof: "persisted_external_ref";
        noProviderPost: true;
    };
} | {
    kind: "not_dispatched";
    effectMayHaveSucceeded: false;
    refusal: FulfillmentRefusal;
} | {
    kind: "unknown";
    effectMayHaveSucceeded: true;
    refusal: FulfillmentRefusal;
}));
export type FulfillmentDispatchPrepareResult = {
    kind: "prepared";
    evidence: DispatchEvidence;
} | {
    kind: "refused";
    refusal: FulfillmentRefusal;
};
export interface FulfillmentDispatchPort {
    /**
     * Without external submission, build the actual command and fingerprint its bytes
     * before store claim/begin. Return only sanitized evidence; keep the raw command
     * transient inside the adapter. Refuse not_dispatchable or the exact selection
     * refusal when preparation cannot proceed. A contact_stale remap requires a new
     * prepare of the rebuilt request/command before another claim/begin.
     */
    prepare(request: FulfillmentDispatchRequest): Promise<FulfillmentDispatchPrepareResult>;
    /**
     * Send exactly the prepared command identified by this evidence. Never silently
     * rebuild a different command; absent/mismatched preparation refuses before I/O.
     * Absent/mismatched preparation: not_dispatchable, phase dispatch, retryable false.
     * Unknown submission outcomes require readback, never blind re-submission.
     */
    dispatch(request: FulfillmentDispatchRequest, evidence: DispatchEvidence): Promise<FulfillmentDispatchResult>;
}
export {};
```

```ts
        kind: "refused";

        document: LabelDocument | null;

}
```

## dist/fulfillment/contracts/observation.d.ts

```ts
import type { NormalizedFulfillmentFact } from "@openlup/core/fulfillment";
import type { CanonicalLocalFulfillmentStatus } from "./index.js";
import type { FulfillmentRefusal } from "./dispatch.js";
/** Explicit adopter policy, forwarded through each durable observation projection. */
export interface FulfillmentObservationCodecs {
    approveReference?(value: string): boolean;
    /** Overrides the query/fragment default; HTTPS, no credentials and URL approval still apply. */
    trackingUrl?(url: string): string | null;
}
export interface FulfillmentTrackingReference {
    trackingNumber: string; /** Normalized carrier kind, never a raw provider code. */
    carrierRef: string | null;
    serviceRef: string | null;
    publicUrl: string | null;
}
/** Durable normalized envelope. eventKey, not the receivedAt-bearing fact fingerprint, deduplicates retries. */
export interface FulfillmentObservation {
    fact: NormalizedFulfillmentFact<CanonicalLocalFulfillmentStatus>;
    eventKind: "shipment.accepted" | "order.processing_started" | "order.picked" | "order.shipped" | "order.delivered" | "parcel.exception" | "parcel.cancelled";
    eventKey: string;
    firstReceivedAt: string;
    correlation: {
        orderRef: string | null;
        fulfillmentRef: string | null;
        externalRef: string | null;
        orderNumber: string | null;
        providerFulfillmentRef: string | null;
        parcelRef: string | null;
    };
    trackingRefs: readonly FulfillmentTrackingReference[];
    /** Original provider status tokens; canonical status does not identify an occurrence. */
    providerStatus?: {
        primary: string | null;
        subStatus: string | null;
    };
    diagnostics?: {
        redactedFieldCount?: number;
        admission?: {
            rejectedTrackingRefs: number;
            rejectedTrackingUrls: number;
        };
        refusal?: FulfillmentRefusal;
        stockContention?: {
            sqlState: "55P03";
            phase: "provider_stock_consumed";
            retryable: true;
        };
        annotation?: "recorded" | "failed";
        recoveredPickedReceipt?: {
            receiptRef: string;
            closed: boolean;
        };
    };
}
export interface FulfillmentObservationPort {
    /** Authenticate untouched bytes before parsing. Inputs live only during verification. */
    verifyAndNormalize(input: {
        rawBody: Uint8Array;
        headers: Readonly<Record<string, string>>;
        receivedAt: string;
    }): Promise<{
        kind: "verified";
        observation: FulfillmentObservation;
    } | {
        kind: "refused";
        refusal: FulfillmentRefusal;
        diagnostics?: {
            invalidTimestamp?: true;
            rejectedCorrelationRefs?: number;
        };
    }>;
}
```

```ts
import type { FulfillmentNativeUnsupported } from './nativeUnsupported.js';
/** Host transaction inputs. actorUserId and idempotencyKey are required for attribution and replay receipts. */
interface MutationInput {
    idempotencyKey: string;
    actorUserId: string;
    metadata?: Record<string, unknown>;
}
interface ParcelMutationInput extends MutationInput {
    fulfillmentOrderId: string;
}
type AdminCommerceFulfillmentCreateRequest = MutationInput & {
    orderId: string;
};
type AdminCommerceFulfillmentRecordProviderAttemptRequest = ParcelMutationInput & {
    providerKind: string;
    status: 'recorded' | 'succeeded' | 'failed';
    requestPayload?: Record<string, unknown>;
    responsePayload?: Record<string, unknown>;
    error?: string | null;
};
type AdminCommerceFulfillmentRecordLabelRequest = ParcelMutationInput & {
    providerKind: string;
    providerTrackingId: string;
    labelUrl?: string | null;
    deliveryEstimateDays?: number | null;
    rawProviderPayload?: Record<string, unknown>;
};
type AdminCommerceFulfillmentHandOffRequest = ParcelMutationInput;
type AdminCommerceFulfillmentTrackingEventRequest = ParcelMutationInput & {
    status: 'in_transit' | 'delivered' | 'exception';
    providerTrackingId?: string;
    rawEvent?: Record<string, unknown>;
};
type AdminCommerceFulfillmentCancelRequest = ParcelMutationInput & {
    reason: string;
};
export interface FulfillmentOperatorMutationResult {
    contractVersion: 'commerce.fulfillment.v0';
    fulfillmentOrderId: string;
    orderId: string;
    status: 'created' | 'packed' | 'label_pending' | 'label_created' | 'handed_over' | 'in_transit' | 'delivered' | 'exception' | 'cancelled';
    replayed: boolean;
}
export interface FulfillmentOperatorMutationPort {
    createCommerceFulfillmentOrder(request: AdminCommerceFulfillmentCreateRequest & {
        actorUserId: string;
    }): Promise<FulfillmentOperatorMutationResult | FulfillmentNativeUnsupported>;
    recordCommerceFulfillmentProviderAttempt(request: AdminCommerceFulfillmentRecordProviderAttemptRequest & {
        actorUserId: string;
    }): Promise<FulfillmentOperatorMutationResult | FulfillmentNativeUnsupported>;
    recordCommerceFulfillmentLabel(request: AdminCommerceFulfillmentRecordLabelRequest & {
        actorUserId: string;
    }): Promise<FulfillmentOperatorMutationResult | FulfillmentNativeUnsupported>;
    handOffCommerceFulfillmentOrder(request: AdminCommerceFulfillmentHandOffRequest & {
        actorUserId: string;
    }): Promise<FulfillmentOperatorMutationResult | FulfillmentNativeUnsupported>;
    recordCommerceFulfillmentTrackingEvent(request: AdminCommerceFulfillmentTrackingEventRequest & {
        actorUserId: string;
    }): Promise<FulfillmentOperatorMutationResult | FulfillmentNativeUnsupported>;
    cancelCommerceFulfillmentOrder(request: AdminCommerceFulfillmentCancelRequest & {
        actorUserId: string;
    }): Promise<FulfillmentOperatorMutationResult | FulfillmentNativeUnsupported>;
}
export {};
```

```ts
import type { SubscriptionCycleStatus } from '@openlup/core/subscription';

export type FulfillmentPaymentStatus = 'not_started' | PaymentIntentStatus;
export interface FulfillmentCommerceOrder {
    id: string;

    clientId: string | null;
    status: 'created' | 'packed' | 'label_pending' | 'label_created' | 'handed_over' | 'in_transit' | 'delivered' | 'exception' | 'cancelled';
    providerKind: string | null;
    providerTrackingId: string | null;
    shippingAddress: {
        addressId: string;
        clientId: string;
        label: string | null;
        line1: string;
        line2: string | null;
        city: string;
        postalCode: string;
        country: string;
    };
    lines: {
        id: string | null;
        orderItemId: string;
        skuId: string;
        sku: string;
        title: string | null;
        quantity: number;
        inventoryReservationIds: string[];
        productSnapshot: Record<string, unknown>;
    }[];
    payment: {
        paymentIntentId: string;
        paymentStatus: FulfillmentPaymentStatus;
        providerPaymentId: string | null;
    };
    inventory: {
        status: 'not_checked' | 'reserved' | 'missing' | 'released' | 'expired' | 'consumed' | 'review_required';
        reservationId: string | null;
        reservationStatus: 'reserved' | 'consumed' | 'released' | 'expired' | null;
        expiresAt: string | null;
        locationId: string | null;
        locationCode: string | null;
    };
    omsEligibility: {
        allowed: boolean;
        reason: 'order_not_paid' | 'payment_not_succeeded' | 'subscription_cycle_not_paid' | 'active_hold' | 'missing_shipping_address' | 'inventory_review' | 'risk_review' | null;
    };
    latestOperation: {
        id: string;
        type: 'created' | 'packed' | 'provider_attempt_recorded' | 'label_created' | 'handed_over' | 'tracking_event_recorded' | 'cancelled';
        occurredAt: string;
        actorUserId: string | null;
        payload: Record<string, unknown>;
    } | null;
    createdAt: string;
    updatedAt: string;
}
export interface FulfillmentOrdersListRequest {

    pageSize: number;
    status?: FulfillmentCommerceOrder['status'];
}
export interface FulfillmentOrderDetailRequest {

    orderId?: string;
}
export interface FulfillmentOrdersListResponse {
    contractVersion: 'commerce.fulfillment.v0';
    orders: FulfillmentCommerceOrder[];
    totalCount: number;
    page: number;
    pageSize: number;
}
export interface FulfillmentOrderDetailResponse {
    contractVersion: 'commerce.fulfillment.v0';
    order: FulfillmentCommerceOrder;
}
/** Host-owned persisted projections. Payment statuses already come from the payments projection. */
export interface FulfillmentCommerceReadPort {
    listCommerceFulfillmentOrders(request: FulfillmentOrdersListRequest): Promise<FulfillmentOrdersListResponse | FulfillmentNativeUnsupported>;
    getCommerceFulfillmentOrderDetail(request: FulfillmentOrderDetailRequest): Promise<FulfillmentOrderDetailResponse | null | FulfillmentNativeUnsupported>;
}
export interface FulfillmentShipmentsOverviewRequest {
    shippedFilter: 'today' | '7d' | '30d' | 'all';
}
/** Bucket rows are opaque host data; native shipment counts cannot replace these buckets. */
export interface FulfillmentShipmentsOverviewResponse {
    approved: Record<string, unknown>[];
    packing: Record<string, unknown>[];
    shippedToday: number;
    shipped: Record<string, unknown>[];
}
export interface FulfillmentShipmentsOverviewPort {
    getShipmentsOverview(request: FulfillmentShipmentsOverviewRequest): Promise<FulfillmentShipmentsOverviewResponse | FulfillmentNativeUnsupported>;
}
/** Existing commerce eligibility remains host-owned; the read model only supplies its facts. */
export interface FulfillmentCommerceReadPolicy {
    evaluateCommerceFulfillmentEligibility(input: {
        orderMode: 'one_time' | 'subscription_cycle';
        orderStatus: FulfillmentOrderStatus;
        paymentStatus: FulfillmentPaymentStatus;
        subscriptionCycleStatus: SubscriptionCycleStatus | null;
        activeHoldCount: number;
        hasShippingAddress: boolean;
        inventoryStatus: FulfillmentCommerceOrder['inventory']['status'];
    }): FulfillmentCommerceOrder['omsEligibility'];
}
export type { FulfillmentProgressInput, FulfillmentProgressPresentation } from "@openlup/core/fulfillment";
```

```ts
import type { FulfillmentObservation } from "./observation.js";
import type { FulfillmentRefusal } from "./dispatch.js";
export type FulfillmentReadbackResult = {
    kind: "found";
    externalRef: string;
    createdAt: string | null;
    updatedAt: string | null;
    observation: FulfillmentObservation;
} | {
    kind: "absent";
    scope: {
        providerRef: string;
        requestKey: string;
        fulfillmentRef: string;
        externalRef: string | null;
    };
    proof: {
        kind: "authoritative_exact_lookup";
        evidenceRef: `evidence:${string}`;
        checkedAt: string;
    };
} | {
    kind: "unknown";
    refusal: FulfillmentRefusal;
} | {
    kind: "unsupported";
    refusal: FulfillmentRefusal;
};
export interface FulfillmentReadbackPort {
    /** Empty pages and missing local rows cannot certify external absence. */
    read(input: {
        providerRef: string;
        requestKey: string;
        fulfillmentRef: string;
        externalRef: string | null;
        mode: "command" | "parcel";
    }): Promise<FulfillmentReadbackResult>;
}
/** Collection observation is separate from authoritative exact lookup. */
export type FulfillmentReconciliationObservation = FulfillmentReadbackResult | {
    kind: "unmapped";
    externalRef: string | null;
    evidence: Pick<FulfillmentObservation, "correlation" | "eventKey" | "providerStatus" | "diagnostics">;
};
export interface FulfillmentObservationPagePort {
    readPage(input: {
        page: number;
        size: number;
    }): Promise<{
        kind: "page";
        rows: readonly FulfillmentReconciliationObservation[];
    } | {
        kind: "page_exhausted";
    } | {
        kind: "failed";
        refusal: FulfillmentRefusal;
        error?: import("./dispatch.js").FulfillmentOperationError;
    }>;
}
```

## dist/fulfillment/contracts/store.d.ts

```ts
import type { DispatchEvidence, FulfillmentDispatchRequest, FulfillmentDispatchResult, FulfillmentRefusal } from "./dispatch.js";
export interface DispatchRecord {
    dispatchRef: string;
    requestKey: string;
    fulfillmentRef: string;
    orderRef: string;
    parcelRef: string | null;
    externalRef: string | null;
    mode: "shadow" | "stage" | "live";
    status: "draft" | "submitting" | "created" | "uncertain" | "failed" | "cancel_requested" | "cancelled";
    evidence: DispatchEvidence;
    replayed: boolean;
    acceptance?: Extract<FulfillmentDispatchResult, {
        kind: "accepted";
    }> | null;
}
export interface FulfillmentStorePort {
    read(requestKey: string): Promise<DispatchRecord | null>;
    readCandidate(fulfillmentRef: string): Promise<FulfillmentDispatchRequest | null>;
    prepare(fulfillmentRef: string): Promise<{
        disposition: "ready" | "withheld";
        contactRevision: number | null;
    }>;
    /** Evidence comes from adapter prepare, before any external submission. */
    claim(input: {
        requestKey: string;
        fulfillmentRef: string;
        orderRef: string;
        parcelRef: string | null;
        mode: DispatchRecord["mode"];
        evidence: DispatchEvidence;
    }): Promise<{
        kind: "claimed";
        record: DispatchRecord;
    } | {
        kind: "refused";
        refusal: FulfillmentRefusal;
    }>;
    /** G14: idempotent mapped attempt; persist only prepared, sanitized evidence. */
    recordProviderAttempt(attemptKey: string, fulfillmentRef: string, preparedEvidence: DispatchEvidence, mode: DispatchRecord["mode"], status: "recorded"): Promise<{
        replayed: boolean;
    }>;
    /** Bind submission to the same prepared command fingerprint recorded by claim. */
    begin(input: {
        dispatchRef: string;
        commandFingerprint: string;
    }): Promise<{
        kind: "begun";
        record: DispatchRecord;
        begun: boolean;
        replayed: boolean;
    } | {
        kind: "refused";
        refusal: FulfillmentRefusal;
    }>;
    /** Accepted rescue retains externalRef as uncertain; only acknowledge closes the local acceptance atomically. */
    finalize(input: {
        dispatchRef: string;
        result: FulfillmentDispatchResult;
    }): Promise<DispatchRecord>;
    acknowledge(input: {
        dispatchRef: string;
        acceptance: Extract<FulfillmentDispatchResult, {
            kind: "accepted";
        }>;
    }): Promise<{
        acknowledged: boolean;
        replayed: boolean;
        record: DispatchRecord;
    }>;
    markStaleSubmissionsUncertain(staleBefore: string): Promise<number>;
}
export interface FulfillmentPaidHandoffPort {
    ensureObligation(input: {
        requestKey: string;
        orderRef: string;
        paidFactRef: string;
    }): Promise<{
        fulfillmentRef: string;
        replayed: boolean;
    }>;
    acknowledge(input: {
        requestKey: string;
        fulfillmentRef: string;
    }): Promise<{
        acknowledged: boolean;
        replayed: boolean;
    }>;
}
```

## dist/fulfillment/contracts/sync.d.ts

```ts
import type { FulfillmentRefusal } from "./dispatch.js";
/** Collection identity never masquerades as a monotonic provider revision. */
interface SyncIdentity {
    providerRef: string;
    syncRunId: string;
    collectedAt: string;
    sourceRevision: string | null;
    revisionSource: "provider" | "collection" | "unknown";
}
export interface StockSnapshot extends SyncIdentity {
    skuRef: string;
    quantityKind: "snapshot";
    totalQuantity: number;
    forSaleQuantity: number;
    reservedOrUnavailableQuantity: number;
}
export interface StockMovement extends SyncIdentity {
    skuRef: string;
    quantityKind: "movement";
    quantity: number;
    occurredAt: string | null;
    operationType: string | null;
    documentRef: string | null;
    lotRef: string | null;
    expirationDate: string | null;
}
export interface SyncCursor {
    status: "idle" | "running" | "succeeded" | "failed";
    lastStockSyncedAt: string | null;
    lastMovementOccurredAt: string | null;
    /** Sanitized continuation only; no credentials, URLs or arbitrary provider payload. */
    continuation: {
        nextRef: string | null;
        startLocalDate: string | null;
        endLocalDate: string | null;
    };
    /** G15: successful sellable coverage gaps; legacy absent values read as []. */
    coverageGapSkus?: readonly string[];
    refusal: FulfillmentRefusal | null;
    /** Per-entry bounded diagnostics and run evidence in the existing cursor JSON, never raw errors. */
    syncRunId?: string;
    counts?: {
        checked: number;
        updated: number;
        movements: number;
        rejected: number;
        failures: number;
        providerCalls: number;
        readBacks: number;
    };
    items?: readonly {
        kind?: "stock" | "movement";
        itemIndex: number;
        skuRef: string | null;
        status: "recorded" | "failed" | "rejected";
        reason?: string;
        activeReserved?: number;
        sellableNow?: number | null;
        coverageGapQuantity?: number;
    }[];
    alerts?: readonly {
        skuRef: string;
        thresholdKind: Exclude<Threshold, "forecast">;
        severity: "info" | "warning" | "critical";
        providerForSaleQuantity: number;
        localAvailableQuantity: number;
        detectedAt: string;
        activeReserved?: number;
        coverageGapQuantity?: number;
        gapHours?: number;
        thresholdHours?: number;
    }[];
    staleSync?: {
        gapHours: number;
        thresholdHours: number;
        detectedAt: string;
    };
    error?: {
        reason: string;
        providerError?: {
            operation: "get_stock" | "get_stock_movements";
            status: number | null;
            code: string;
            retryable: boolean;
        };
    } | null;
}
export interface LocalStock {
    skuRef: string;
    onHand: number;
    reserved: number;
    unavailable: number;
    safetyStock: number;
}
type Threshold = "safety_stock" | "reservation_coverage" | "forecast" | "stale_sync";
type Conflict = "unknown_sku" | "ean_conflict" | "kind_quantity_conflict";
export interface FulfillmentStockSyncProvider {
    /** Rejected rows carry only their input index and a closed reason, never an unsafe reference. */
    getStock(): Promise<(StockSnapshot | {
        kind: "rejected";
        itemIndex: number;
        reason: "invalid_sku_ref";
    })[]>;
    getStockMovements(input?: {
        startLocalDate?: string;
        endLocalDate?: string;
        size?: number;
    }): Promise<(StockMovement | {
        kind: "rejected";
        itemIndex: number;
        reason: "invalid_sku_ref";
    })[]>;
}
export interface FulfillmentStockSyncPort {
    readCursor(): Promise<SyncCursor | null>;
    /** G16: unaggregated lines at the worker clock; quantity retains the four local balances. */
    readLocalInventoryStock(requestedAt: string): Promise<readonly {
        skuRef: string;
        quantity: Pick<LocalStock, "onHand" | "reserved" | "unavailable" | "safetyStock">;
        fulfillable: boolean;
        locationStatus: "active" | "inactive";
        lotStatus: "available" | "quarantined" | "expired" | "recalled" | null;
        expiresAt: string | null;
    }[]>;
    readSkuInventoryClasses(skus: string[]): Promise<Map<string, "sellable" | "packaging" | null>>;
    readActiveReservations(skus: string[], requestedAt: string): Promise<Map<string, number>>;
    recordCursor(input: SyncCursor): Promise<void>;
    recordStockSnapshot(input: {
        requestKey: string;
        snapshot: StockSnapshot;
        local: LocalStock;
        mismatchKind: "none";
        inventoryClass: "sellable" | "packaging" | null;
    }): Promise<{
        replayed: boolean;
        readBack: boolean;
    }>;
    recordProviderStockCurrent(input: {
        requestKey: string;
        snapshot: StockSnapshot;
        inventoryClass: "sellable" | "packaging" | null;
        lastSyncedAt: string;
        staleAfter: string;
    }): Promise<{
        replayed: boolean;
        readBack: boolean;
    }>;
    recordMovement(input: {
        requestKey: string;
        movement: StockMovement;
    }): Promise<{
        replayed: boolean;
        readBack: boolean;
    }>;
    recordLowStockEvidence(input: {
        requestKey: string;
        skuRef: string;
        thresholdKind: Exclude<Threshold, "forecast">;
        severity: "info" | "warning" | "critical";
        providerForSaleQuantity: number;
        localAvailableQuantity: number;
        syncRunId: string;
    }): Promise<{
        replayed: boolean;
    }>;
    resolveLowStockEvidence(input: {
        skuRef: string;
        activeThresholdKinds: Threshold[];
        syncRunId: string;
    }): Promise<{
        resolved: number;
    }>;
}
interface ProductPack {
    ean: string;
    quantity: number;
}
export interface FulfillmentProductSyncProvider {
    /** null means skipped (no private push metadata), never a newly created local SKU. */
    ensureProduct(sku: string): Promise<{
        replayed: boolean;
    } | null>;
    getProductPacks(sku: string): Promise<ProductPack[]>;
    addProductPack(sku: string, pack: ProductPack): Promise<void>;
    listStockedSkus(): Promise<string[]>;
}
export interface FulfillmentProductSyncPort {
    listLocalProductPacks(): Promise<{
        sku: string;
        packs: (ProductPack & {
            kind: "unit" | "collective";
        })[];
    }[]>;
    markPackSynced(input: {
        sku: string;
        ean: string;
    }): Promise<void>;
    upsertPulledPack(input: {
        sku: string;
        ean: string;
        quantity: number;
        kind: "unit" | "collective";
    }): Promise<{
        conflict: "none" | "local_locked_mismatch" | "ean_owned_by_other_sku" | "sku_not_found";
        replayed: boolean;
    }>;
    recordReconciliationEvidence(input: {
        requestKey: string;
        sku: string;
        ean: string | null;
        conflictKind: Conflict;
        providerQuantity: number;
        identity: SyncIdentity;
        refusal: FulfillmentRefusal | null;
    }): Promise<{
        replayed: boolean;
    }>;
    resolveReconciliationEvidence(input: {
        sku: string;
        activeConflictKinds: Conflict[];
        syncRunId: string;
    }): Promise<{
        resolved: number;
    }>;
}
export {};
```

```ts
import type { FulfillmentObservation } from "./contracts/index.js";
/** Ledger effects for an already accepted observation; acceptance and retry belong to the caller. */
interface FulfillmentObservationPort<TrackingStatus> {
    markProviderStockConsumed(input: {
        idempotencyKey: string;
        fulfillmentOrderId: string;
    }): Promise<{
        replayed: boolean;
    }>;
    markHandedOver(input: {
        idempotencyKey: string;
        fulfillmentOrderId: string;
        suppressDispatched: boolean;
    }): Promise<{
        replayed: boolean;
    }>;
    recordTrackingReference(input: {
        idempotencyKey: string;
        orderId: string;
        fulfillmentOrderId: string;
        trackingNumber: string;
        trackingUrl: string | null;
        carrierKind: string | null;
        service: string | null;
        status: TrackingStatus;
        observation: FulfillmentObservation;
    }): Promise<{
        replayed: boolean;
        readBack: boolean;
    }>;
}
interface FulfillmentObservationTrackingReference {
    trackingNumber: string;
    trackingUrl?: string | null;
    carrierKind?: string | null;
    service?: string | null;
}
interface FulfillmentObservationInput<LocalStatus, TrackingStatus> {
    port: FulfillmentObservationPort<TrackingStatus>;
    localStatus: LocalStatus;
    ids: {
        orderId: string;
        fulfillmentOrderId: string;
    };
    observation: FulfillmentObservation;
    trackingRefs: readonly FulfillmentObservationTrackingReference[];
    stockIdempotencyKey: string;
    handoffIdempotencyKey: string;
    trackingIdempotencyKey: (trackingNumber: string) => string;
    issueAccountingInvoice?: () => Promise<void>;
    onReplayed?: () => void;
    onTrackingRecorded?: (result: {
        replayed: boolean;
        readBack: boolean;
    }) => void;
}
/** Pure status decisions/projection; the mechanism owns every effect and await. */
interface FulfillmentObservationStatusPolicy<LocalStatus, TrackingStatus> {
    consumesStock(status: LocalStatus): boolean;
    handsOver(status: LocalStatus): boolean;
    suppressDispatched(status: LocalStatus): boolean;
    trackingStatus(status: LocalStatus): TrackingStatus;
}
/** Stock → durable handover → every tracking reference → optional invoice. No catches or retries. */
export declare function applyFulfillmentObservationEffects<LocalStatus, TrackingStatus>(input: FulfillmentObservationInput<LocalStatus, TrackingStatus>, policy: FulfillmentObservationStatusPolicy<LocalStatus, TrackingStatus>): Promise<void>;
export {};
```
