import type { FulfillmentNativeUnsupported } from './contracts/nativeUnsupported.js';
export interface FulfillmentHandlerRequest {
    method?: string;
    query: Record<string, string | string[] | undefined>;
    body?: unknown;
}
export interface FulfillmentHandlerResponder<Response> {
    sendMethodNotAllowed(res: Response, methods: readonly string[]): void;
    sendBffSuccess(res: Response, data: unknown): void;
    sendBffError(res: Response, code: 'UNAUTHORIZED' | 'FORBIDDEN' | 'BAD_REQUEST' | 'INVALID_RESPONSE' | 'NOT_FOUND' | 'CONFLICT' | 'UPSTREAM_UNAVAILABLE', message: string, options?: {
        details: unknown;
    }): void;
}
export type AdminAuthResult = {
    ok: false;
    code: 'UNAUTHORIZED' | 'FORBIDDEN';
    message: string;
} | {
    ok: true;
    userId: string;
};
export interface BaseDeps<Response> {
    authorizeAdmin(req: FulfillmentHandlerRequest): Promise<AdminAuthResult>;
    respond: FulfillmentHandlerResponder<Response>;
}
export declare function isNativeUnsupported(value: unknown): value is FulfillmentNativeUnsupported;
export declare class CommerceFulfillmentConflictError extends Error {
    readonly details: Record<string, unknown>;
    constructor(message?: string, details?: Record<string, unknown>);
}
export declare function authorize<Response>(req: FulfillmentHandlerRequest, res: Response, authorizeAdmin: BaseDeps<Response>['authorizeAdmin'], respond: FulfillmentHandlerResponder<Response>): Promise<{
    userId: string;
} | null>;

/** Read-only evidence boundary; parsing remains with the owning host schema. */
export declare function createFulfillmentLowStockEvidenceHandler<Request extends {
    method?: string;
    query: Record<string, string | string[] | undefined>;
}, Response, Query, Result>({ evidencePort, authorizeAdmin, requestSchema, responseSchema, respond }: {
    evidencePort: {
        getLowStockEvidence(query: Query): Promise<unknown>;
    };
    authorizeAdmin(request: Request): Promise<boolean>;
    requestSchema: {
        safeParse(input: unknown): {
            success: true;
            data: Query;
        } | {
            success: false;
            error: {
                flatten(): unknown;
            };
        };
    };
    responseSchema: {
        safeParse(input: unknown): {
            success: true;
            data: Result;
        } | {
            success: false;
        };
    };
    respond: {
        sendBffError(res: Response, code: "UPSTREAM_UNAVAILABLE" | "UNAUTHORIZED" | "BAD_REQUEST" | "INVALID_RESPONSE", message: string, options?: {
            details: unknown;
        }): void;
        sendBffSuccess(res: Response, data: Result): void;
        sendMethodNotAllowed(res: Response, methods: readonly string[]): void;
    };
}): (req: Request, res: Response) => Promise<void>;

```ts
type PaidFulfillmentRecoveryAction = "wait_for_outbox" | "requeue_discarded_order_paid_outbox" | "inspect_processed_without_effect" | "inspect_missing_order_paid_outbox" | "monitor_dispatch" | "inspect_missing_dispatch_ref" | "inspect_stale_dispatch_submission" | "inspect_uncertain_dispatch" | "inspect_failed_dispatch" | "inspect_created_dispatch_without_provider_order_id" | "inspect_terminal_dispatch" | "inspect_unknown_dispatch_status";
type PaidFulfillmentRecoveryReason = "paid_order_without_fulfillment_order" | "order_paid_outbox_missing" | "order_paid_outbox_discarded" | "order_paid_outbox_processed_without_fulfillment" | "provider_dispatch_in_progress" | "provider_fulfillment_without_dispatch_ref" | "provider_dispatch_submission_stale" | "provider_dispatch_outcome_uncertain" | "provider_dispatch_failed" | "provider_dispatch_created_without_provider_order_id" | "provider_dispatch_terminal_without_progress" | "provider_dispatch_status_unknown";
type PaidFulfillmentRecoveryPosture = "automatic_local_requeue_safe" | "wait_for_existing_automation" | "operator_review_required";
type PaidFulfillmentOrderRow = Record<string, unknown> & {
    id: string;
    status?: string | null;
    mode?: string | null;
    created_at?: string | null;
    updated_at?: string | null;
};
type PaidFulfillmentOrderEvidenceRow = Record<string, unknown> & {
    id: string;
    order_id: string;
    provider_kind?: string | null;
    status?: string | null;
    created_at?: string | null;
    updated_at?: string | null;
};
type PaidFulfillmentDispatchRefRow = Record<string, unknown> & {
    id?: string | null;
    fulfillment_order_id: string;
    provider_order_id?: string | null;
    status?: string | null;
    created_at?: string | null;
    updated_at?: string | null;
};
type PaidFulfillmentOutboxEventRow = Record<string, unknown> & {
    id: string;
    event_type: string;
    status: string;
    aggregate_id?: string | null;
    created_at?: string | null;
    updated_at?: string | null;
    available_at?: string | null;
    attempts?: number | null;
};
type PaidFulfillmentRecoveryCandidate = {
    orderId: string;
    orderStatus: string;
    orderMode: string | null;
    fulfillmentOrderId: string | null;
    providerKind: string | null;
    dispatchRefId: string | null;
    dispatchStatus: string | null;
    outboxEventId: string | null;
    outboxStatus: string | null;
    outboxAttempts: number | null;
    reason: PaidFulfillmentRecoveryReason;
    recommendedAction: PaidFulfillmentRecoveryAction;
    recoveryPosture: PaidFulfillmentRecoveryPosture;
    ageSeconds: number;
};
type PaidFulfillmentRecoveryPlan = {
    checkedOrders: number;
    candidates: PaidFulfillmentRecoveryCandidate[];
};
export declare function collectPaidFulfillmentRecoveryCandidates(input: {
    providerRef: string;
    orders: PaidFulfillmentOrderRow[];
    fulfillmentOrders: PaidFulfillmentOrderEvidenceRow[];
    dispatchRefs?: PaidFulfillmentDispatchRefRow[];
    orderPaidOutboxEvents: PaidFulfillmentOutboxEventRow[];
    now: Date;
    minimumAgeSeconds?: number;
    dispatchSubmissionStaleSeconds?: number;
}): PaidFulfillmentRecoveryPlan;
export {};
```

## dist/fulfillment/providerCommandLedger.d.ts

```ts
/** Structural protocol for the contributed durable ledger. Each operation must
 * be one atomic store transaction, including its transition evidence. P4 owns SQL.
 * This factory holds no leases, retry counters, commands or persistence in memory.
 */
type CommandStatus = "queued" | "claimed" | "submitting" | "accepted" | "confirmed" | "retry_wait" | "uncertain" | "manual_review" | "terminal_rejected" | "cancelled";
type CommandResult = {
    commandId: string;
    status: CommandStatus;
    version: number;
    applied: boolean;
};
type Fence = {
    commandId: string;
    claimToken: string;
    claimGeneration: number;
    expectedVersion: number;
};
type Proof = {
    kind: "provider_reference" | "correlation_key" | "provider_idempotency_key";
    fingerprint: string;
    observedAt: string;
    source: "provider_lookup" | "provider_webhook" | "operator_verified";
};
type ClaimedCommand = {
    commandId: string;
    fulfillmentRef: string;
    orderRef: string;
    providerRef: string;
    commandKind: "dispatch_create";
    contractVersion: "1";
    status: "claimed";
    version: number;
    commandKeyFingerprint: string;
    commandPayloadFingerprint: string;
    providerIdempotencyKeyFingerprint: string | null;
    routingFingerprint: string;
    configurationFingerprint: string;
    correlationKeyFingerprint: string;
    retryBudget: number;
    retryCount: number;
    claimToken: string;
    claimGeneration: number;
    leaseExpiresAt: string;
};
type LedgerStore = {
    enqueue(input: {
        fulfillmentRef: string;
        providerRef: string;
        commandKind: "dispatch_create";
        contractVersion: "1";
        idempotencyKey: string;
        commandKeyFingerprint: string;
        commandPayloadFingerprint: string;
        providerIdempotencyKeyFingerprint: string | null;
        routingFingerprint: string;
        configurationFingerprint: string;
        correlationKeyFingerprint: string;
        retryBudget: number;
    }): Promise<CommandResult>;
    claim(input: {
        providerRef: string;
        commandKind: "dispatch_create";
        limit: number;
        claimOwnerFingerprint: string;
        leaseSeconds: number;
    }): Promise<readonly ClaimedCommand[]>;
    markSubmitting(input: Fence & {
        providerAttemptRef: string | null;
    }): Promise<CommandResult>;
    applyWriteOutcome(input: Fence & {
        identityFingerprint: string;
        providerAttemptRef: string | null;
    } & ({
        kind: "accepted";
        reason: "provider_accepted";
        providerReferenceFingerprint: string;
    } | {
        kind: "uncertain";
        reason: "write_uncertain";
    } | {
        kind: "rejected";
        reason: "safe_rejection" | "terminal_rejection";
        retryNotBefore: string | null;
        proof: {
            kind: "definitive_provider_rejection";
            fingerprint: string;
            observedAt: string;
            source: "provider_response";
        };
    })): Promise<CommandResult>;
    confirm(input: Fence & {
        identityFingerprint: string;
        providerAttemptRef: string | null;
        proof: Proof;
    }): Promise<CommandResult>;
    reconcile(input: {
        commandId: string;
        expectedVersion: number;
        identityFingerprint: string;
        proof: Proof;
    }): Promise<CommandResult & {
        proofRecorded: boolean;
    }>;
    resolve(input: {
        commandId: string;
        expectedVersion: number;
        identityFingerprint: string;
    } & ({
        decision: "manual_review";
        reason: "effect_unknown" | "identity_mismatch" | "invalid_contract" | "unsafe_retry";
        proof: Proof;
    } | {
        decision: "retry_wait";
        reason: "authoritative_absence";
        retryNotBefore: string;
        proof: {
            kind: "authoritative_absence";
            fingerprint: string;
            observedAt: string;
            source: "provider_lookup";
        };
    })): Promise<CommandResult & {
        proofRecorded: boolean;
    }>;
    release(input: Fence & ({
        action: "release";
        reason: "command_enqueued";
    } | {
        action: "cancel";
        reason: "operator_cancelled";
    })): Promise<CommandResult>;
    recoverExpired(input: {
        limit: number;
    }): Promise<readonly CommandResult[]>;
};
/** Bind the durable protocol without substituting a local state machine.
 * Store fences compare token, generation, version and unexpired lease together.
 * Expired claimed work becomes queued; expired submitting/accepted work becomes
 * uncertain. Only authoritative scoped absence may admit uncertain work to retry.
 * Claim respects availability/retry budget; finalization cannot invent absence.
 */
export declare function createFulfillmentCommandLedger(store: LedgerStore): LedgerStore;
export {};
```

} | {

    mode: "shadow" | "stage" | "live";

} | {

import type { HttpRequest, HttpResponse } from "./types.js";
export declare const NODE_HTTP_TIMEOUT_CODE = "NODE_HTTP_TIMEOUT";
export interface HttpDeadlineOptions {
    req: HttpRequest;
    res: HttpResponse;
    durationMs: number;
    run: () => Promise<unknown> | unknown;
    onLateError?: (error: unknown) => void;
}
/**
 * Run one Node HTTP admission from the instant before lazy loading/adaptation.
 *
 * A timeout before headers commit gets one minimal JSON 504. After headers are
 * committed we cannot change the status safely, so the response is destroyed.
 * The invocation promise remains observed, preventing a late rejection from
 * becoming an unhandled rejection or a second response.
 */
export declare function runWithHttpDeadline(options: HttpDeadlineOptions): Promise<void>;
/** Send a response before closing its request input, so the status is deliverable. */
export declare function closeInputAfterResponse(req: HttpRequest, res: HttpResponse): void;

import type { IncomingMessage, ServerResponse } from "node:http";
import type { HttpRequest, HttpResponse } from "./types.js";
export declare const DEFAULT_NODE_BUFFERED_BODY_LIMIT_BYTES = 1048576;
export declare class NodeRequestBodyTooLargeError extends Error {
    constructor();
}
export declare class NodeRequestAbortedError extends Error {
    constructor();
}
/** A path is a webhook iff it contains a `/webhooks/` segment. */
export declare function isWebhookPath(pathname: string): boolean;
/**
 * The PLATFORM's stream-first standalone entrypoints: they read the raw stream
 * themselves, so the runtime must never pre-parse their body.
 *
 * A deployment's own stream-first endpoint is NOT listed here. It declares
 * `rawStream: true` on its `HttpEntrypointRegistration` and the runtime passes that
 * decision to `adaptRequest` directly — which is what stops this publishable list
 * from having to name a path only that deployment owns.
 */
export declare function isRawStreamEntrypoint(pathname: string): boolean;
export declare function pathnameOf(req: IncomingMessage): string;
/**
 * Decorate a Node response with Vercel's status/json/send/redirect sugar (productionized from the
 * dev shim's decorateResponse — identical semantics).
 */
export declare function decorateResponse(res: ServerResponse): HttpResponse;
/**
 * Adapt a Node IncomingMessage into the Vercel request shape, honoring the raw-body invariant:
 *  - raw-stream entrypoints (process/csp/feedback): stream left intact, NO req.body;
 *  - everything else: raw bytes buffered + re-exposed as async-iterable (so readRawBody sees the
 *    EXACT bytes for any signature-verifying route, webhook-segment or not) AND req.body = parsed JSON.
 */
export declare function adaptRequest(req: IncomingMessage, pathname: string,
/** Explicit override for a caller that knows the entrypoint's body contract (see above). */
rawStream?: boolean, bufferedBodyLimitBytes?: number): Promise<HttpRequest>;
/** Narrow a raw IncomingMessage to HttpRequest at the server boundary (query/cookies set in adapt). */
export declare function decorateRequest(req: IncomingMessage): HttpRequest;

## dist/http/runtime.d.ts

```ts
import { type Server } from "node:http";
import type { HttpRuntimePort } from "@openlup/core/platform-runtime";
import type { EntrypointLoader } from "./types.js";
import type { BffDispatch, RouteEntry } from "../bff/index.js";
import { type NodeSecurityHeaderPolicy, type StaticAssetReader } from "./security.js";
/** One deployment-specific standalone entrypoint, matched by its exact path. */
export interface HttpEntrypointRegistration {
    path: string;
    rawStream?: boolean;
    maxDurationMs?: number;
    load: EntrypointLoader;
}
/**
 * One deployment-specific entrypoint matched by a path rule. `match` returns the query values the
 * entrypoint receives for this path, or null when the path is not its own. The body is buffered.
 */
export interface HttpPathEntrypoint {
    match(pathname: string): Record<string, string> | null;
    load: EntrypointLoader;
    /** Request deadline; defaults to 10 seconds. */
    maxDurationMs?: number;
}
export interface NodeLegacyRedirect {
    source: string;
    destination: string;
    permanent: boolean;
}
export interface NodeCsrFallback {
    exactPaths: readonly string[];
    routeFamilies: readonly string[];
}
export interface NodeHttpRuntimeOptions {
    /** The deployment's BFF route table. */
    routes: readonly RouteEntry[];
    /** The dispatcher that matches `routes` (see `createBffDispatcher`). */
    dispatch: BffDispatch;
    /** Validated public-route contract supplied by the host composition root. */
    routing?: {
        legacyRedirects: readonly NodeLegacyRedirect[];
        ssgPaths: readonly string[];
        csrFallback: NodeCsrFallback;
    };
    /** Deployment header policy, including the content security policy. */
    security: NodeSecurityHeaderPolicy;
    /** robots.txt body for a request host; without it `/robots.txt` is served like any other path. */
    robotsTxt?: (host: string | undefined) => string;
    /**
     * Platform-path standalone handlers: the CSP report sink at `/api/csp-report` and the hosted
     * BFF router at `/api/bff-router`. A path without a handler is treated like any other path.
     */
    entrypoints?: {
        cspReport?: EntrypointLoader;
        bffRouter?: EntrypointLoader;
    };
    /**
     * Deployment exact-path handlers, checked after the CSP report entrypoint and before the BFF
     * router; a later registration replaces an earlier one with the same path.
     */
    extraEntrypoints?: readonly HttpEntrypointRegistration[];
    /** Deployment path-rule handlers checked before the BFF dispatcher, first match wins. */
    pathEntrypoints?: readonly HttpPathEntrypoint[];
    /** Deployment path-rule handlers checked after legacy redirects and before static documents. */
    fallbackPathEntrypoints?: readonly HttpPathEntrypoint[];
    /** Registry-derived allowlist; undeclared job ids must resolve null. */
    scheduledEntrypoint?: (jobId: string) => EntrypointLoader | null;
    /** Explicit host deadlines; missing cells use the bounded defaults below. */
    durations?: {
        bff?: number;
        cspReport?: number;
        bffRouter?: number;
        scheduled?: (jobId: string) => number;
    };
    /** Static SPA reader; without one, unmatched non-API paths answer 404. */
    staticReader?: StaticAssetReader;
    /** Structured error sink (defaults to console.error). */
    onError?: (context: string, error: unknown) => void;
}
/** Build the runtime's request handler (exported for unit tests; wraps every dispatch path). */
export declare function createRequestHandler(options: NodeHttpRuntimeOptions): HttpRuntimePort["handle"];
/** Create the Node HttpRuntimePort: `handle` for one request, `listen` to bind the server. */
export declare function createNodeHttpRuntime(options: NodeHttpRuntimeOptions): HttpRuntimePort & {
    server: Server | null;
};
```
## dist/http/security.d.ts

```ts
import type { IncomingMessage, ServerResponse } from "node:http";
import type { HttpResponse } from "./types.js";
/** Deployment-owned header policy applied to every response of the Node runtime. */
export interface NodeSecurityHeaderPolicy {
    /** Headers set on every response unless the handler already set them, in this order. */
    readonly globalHeaders: ReadonlyArray<readonly [string, string]>;
    /** Static-asset path prefixes that receive the long-lived asset Cache-Control. */
    readonly assetCachePrefixes: readonly string[];
    /** Path families (the path or anything below it) that receive `X-Robots-Tag: noindex, nofollow`. */
    readonly noindexFamilies: readonly string[];
    /** Exact paths that receive `X-Robots-Tag: noindex, nofollow`. */
    readonly noindexExacts: readonly string[];
    /** True for a production host; every other host receives the strict non-production X-Robots-Tag. */
    readonly isProductionHost: (host: string | undefined) => boolean;
}
/** Apply the deployment's global and path-specific headers for this route. */
export declare function applySecurityHeaders(res: ServerResponse, pathname: string, host: string | undefined, policy: NodeSecurityHeaderPolicy): void;
/** Pluggable static reader so tests can inject an in-memory dist/ without touching the filesystem. */
export interface StaticAssetReader {
    /** Read a built asset (returns null if it does not exist). */
    read(relativePath: string): Promise<{
        body: Buffer;
        contentType: string;
    } | null>;
    /** The SPA fallback document (dist/index.html). */
    readIndex(): Promise<{
        body: Buffer;
        contentType: string;
    }>;
}
export declare function contentTypeFor(pathname: string): string;
/** A path "looks like" a built asset if it has a file extension (so we 404 missing assets, not SPA). */
export declare function looksLikeAsset(pathname: string): boolean;
/**
 * Serve a built SPA asset if present, otherwise the document the build produced for this route,
 * otherwise the declared CSR fallback for client routes. Missing real assets (a hashed bundle that
 * does not exist) and unknown extensionless paths return 404 rather than the SPA shell.
 *
 * The middle step is not decoration. The build writes each pre-rendered route as
 * `<route>/index.html` and writes the HOME document to `index.html`; skipping that lookup hands
 * every pre-rendered URL the home document instead of its own. The hosted platform does the same
 * thing declaratively, one rewrite per route, which is why this host had no equivalent code.
 */
export declare function serveSpaStatic(_req: IncomingMessage, res: HttpResponse, pathname: string, reader: StaticAssetReader, allowRouteDocument?: boolean, allowFallback?: boolean): Promise<void>;
```

## dist/http/types.d.ts

```ts
import type { IncomingMessage, ServerResponse } from "node:http";
/** A Node request decorated with the parsed query, cookies and body. */
export interface HttpRequest extends IncomingMessage {
    query: Record<string, string | string[] | undefined>;
    cookies?: Record<string, string>;
    body?: unknown;
    /** Cooperative cancellation owned by the portable host adapter. */
    abortSignal?: AbortSignal;
}
/** A Node response decorated with the helper methods hosted functions provide. */
export interface HttpResponse extends ServerResponse {
    status(code: number): this;
    json(body: unknown): this;
    send(body: string | Buffer | object): this;
    redirect(url: string): this;
    redirect(status: number, url: string): this;
}
/** A default-export handler shared by every physical API entrypoint. */
export type HttpHandler = (req: HttpRequest, res: HttpResponse) => Promise<unknown> | unknown;
/** A lazily loaded module exposing one default-export handler. */
export type EntrypointLoader = () => Promise<{
    default: HttpHandler;
}>;
```
## dist/http/webhookSignature.d.ts

```ts
import type { HttpRequest } from "./types.js";
/** Hard cap on raw webhook body bytes — the payment provider events are well under 256 KiB. */
export declare const MAX_WEBHOOK_BODY_BYTES = 1048576;
/** Thrown by readRawBody when the streamed body exceeds MAX_WEBHOOK_BODY_BYTES. */
export declare class WebhookBodyTooLargeError extends Error {
    constructor();
}
/**
 * Reads the exact raw request body bytes for provider webhook signature
 * verification.
 *
 * `@vercel/node` (v3, the builder for `/api` functions in this Vite project)
 * auto-parses `application/json` request bodies into `req.body` and IGNORES the
 * `export const config = { api: { bodyParser: false } }` opt-out — that opt-out
 * is a Next.js Pages Router convention, not a vanilla `@vercel/node` one
 * (verified empirically against the hidden-preview deploy: a JSON payload posted
 * with `Content-Type: application/json` arrives parsed, the same bytes posted as
 * `text/plain` arrive raw). Re-serializing the parsed object with
 * `JSON.stringify` does NOT reproduce the provider's exact bytes — the payment provider signs
 * pretty-printed JSON, so a compacted re-serialization breaks HMAC verification.
 *
 * Reading the underlying request stream FIRST — before touching `req.body`,
 * whose getter triggers the parse that consumes the stream — preserves the exact
 * bytes when the platform parses lazily. When `req` is not a stream (unit tests
 * pass a plain `{ body }` object) or the stream yields nothing (already
 * consumed), we fall back to `req.body`.
 */
export declare function readRawBody(req: HttpRequest): Promise<string>;
export declare function verifyHmacSignature(input: {
    rawBody: string;
    signatureHeader: string | undefined;
    secret: string;
}): boolean;
/**
 * Constant-time comparison of two shared-secret strings.
 *
 * Exported because it is not payment-specific: any route holding a shared token has to compare it
 * the same way, and a second hand-rolled copy of a security primitive is how one of them quietly
 * stops being constant-time. The length check comes first because `timingSafeEqual` THROWS on a
 * length mismatch rather than returning false, and the length of a shared token is not the secret.
 */
export declare function timingSafeStringEqual(left: string, right: string): boolean;
```
